[Transfers name to RAM]
;	Bug here: Character setup is called before name validation.
;	Entering an invalid name will cause all default items to be duplicated.
C7/507F:	ADCCA1  	LDA $A1CC
C7/5082:	48      	PHA 			;Push Accumulator.
C7/5083:	C220    	REP #$20      	;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C7/5085:	AD2BA2  	LDA $A22B		;Load [NAME_DIALOG_PC_ID] into Accumulator.
C7/5088:	0A      	ASL A			;Multiply By 2.
C7/5089:	0A      	ASL A			;Multiply By 2.
C7/508A:	8D73A1  	STA $A173
C7/508D:	0A      	ASL A			;Multiply By 2.
C7/508E:	18      	CLC 			;Clear Carry Flag.
C7/508F:	6D73A1  	ADC $A173
C7/5092:	AA      	TAX 			;Transfer the pointer to Register X.
C7/5093:	DA      	PHX 			;Push the pointer into the stack for safe keeping.
C7/5094:	E220    	SEP #$20		;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C7/5096:	A90C    	LDA #$0C		;Load 0x0C into Accumulator.
C7/5098:	9E00CC  	STZ $CC00,X		;Store Zero into [CHARACTER_NAME, X].
C7/509B:	E8      	INX 			;Increment Register X.
C7/509C:	3A      	DEC A			;Decrement Accumulator.
C7/509D:	D0F9    	BNE $5098		;Branch back up and fill name array with zeroes.
C7/509F:	9CCDA1  	STZ $A1CD
C7/50A2:	7A      	PLY 			;Pull the base name pointer into Register Y.
C7/50A3:	C220    	REP #$20      	;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C7/50A5:	A2D200  	LDX #$00D2		;Load 0x00D2 into Register X. (Base address of the entered name array)
C7/50A8:	BD0090  	LDA $9000,X		;Load the character byte.
C7/50AB:	DA      	PHX 			;Push Register X.
C7/50AC:	38      	SEC 			;Set Carry Flag.
C7/50AD:	E95820  	SBC #$2058		;Subtract 0x2058 from the character byte to get the pointer.
C7/50B0:	4A      	LSR A			;Divide By 2.
C7/50B1:	AA      	TAX 			;Transfer the pointer to Register X.
C7/50B2:	E220    	SEP #$20		;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C7/50B4:	BFB274C7	LDA $C774B2,X	;Load the encoded character from [TextData_NamingDialog].
C7/50B8:	9900CC  	STA $CC00,Y		;Store the character into [CHARACTER_NAME, X].
C7/50BB:	C220    	REP #$20      	;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C7/50BD:	C8      	INY 			;Increment Register Y.
C7/50BE:	FA      	PLX 			;Pull Register X.
C7/50BF:	E8      	INX 			;Increment Register X.
C7/50C0:	E8      	INX 			;Increment Register X.
C7/50C1:	CECCA1  	DEC $A1CC
C7/50C4:	D0E2    	BNE $50A8
C7/50C6:	E220    	SEP #$20		;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C7/50C8:	68      	PLA 			;Pull Accumulator.
C7/50C9:	8DCCA1  	STA $A1CC
C7/50CC:	C220    	REP #$20      	;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C7/50CE:	AD2BA2  	LDA $A22B		;Load [NAME_DIALOG_PC_ID] into Accumulator.
C7/50D1:	29FF00  	AND #$00FF		;Drop the high byte.
C7/50D4:	EB      	XBA 			;Switch A & B Accumulators.
C7/50D5:	0A      	ASL A			;Multiply By 2.
C7/50D6:	AA      	TAX 			;Transfer the pointer to Register X. (Pointer to character data)
C7/50D7:	E220    	SEP #$20		;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C7/50D9:	08      	PHP 			;Push Processor Status Register.
C7/50DA:	223900C0	JSR $C00039     ;Jump to MasterDispatchTable->JumpTable_InitializationDispatchTable->InitializeDefaultPlayerCharacterGearAndBaseStatsLong.
C7/50DE:	28      	PLP 			;Pull Processor Status Register.
C7/50DF:	6B      	RTL			    ;Return Long.

[Seems this loads some OpCodes into RAM {58 20} & {5A 20}]
